A people named in the singular with the article (10:16-18; hakkena'ani, 10:18, 19) is an English plural: the Jebusites, the Amorites, the Girgashites, the Hivites, the Arkites, the Sinites, the Arvadites, the Zemarites, the Hamathites, the Canaanites.
- Lists: every "and" that the Hebrew has is kept (10:2 has six), and where it has none the English has none (10:1 "Shem, Ham, and Japheth", as at 5:32; 10:4 two pairs, "Elishah and Tarshish, the Kittim and the Dodanim"). bene X with the names after it "The sons of X were", as at 9:18; uvene "And the sons of" (10:2 and 10:22 have no "And"). A list that runs across verse breaks is one sentence (10:11-12, 13-14, 15-18, 26-29). The aside of 10:14 stands in parentheses (the John 2 line), so that the Caphtorim are heard as fathered by Egypt and not as coming out with the Philistines: the Hebrew marks them as an object of "fathered".
- yalad of a father "fathered" (4:18), also where the one fathered is a people or a city (10:13, 15-18); the niphal and the passive "be born to" (10:1, 21, 25), as at 4:18, 4:26 and 6:1. yullad with no subject (10:21) "sons were born", with "sons" supplied from 10:1; ulShem ... gam-hu "And to Shem also", as at 4:26, with the two descriptions of Shem set next to his name. shem ha'echad ... weshem achiv (10:25) "The name of the one was ..., and his brother's name was", as at 4:19, 21.
- Three verbs are kept apart. parad in the niphal (10:5, 32; it returns at 13:9, 11, 14 and 25:23, and the hiphil at Deut 32:8) "separate": peoples parting from one stock and from each other. puts in the niphal (10:18) "be scattered", as planned (9:19; 11:4, 8, 9). palag in the niphal (10:25) "be divided", with one bracketed note that it sounds like the name "Peleg" (the piel is said of tongues at Ps 55:9; pelaggot is "streams" at Job 20:17). parad shares "separate" with hivdil (1:4-18); 2:10 has "divides" for it, of the river, and hitpared stays "be scattered" at Job 4:11.
- lashon of what a people speaks (10:5, 20, 31) "language"; of the organ it stays "tongue" (Job 6:30). ish lilshono "each according to its language". le- with families, languages, generations and nations "according to", as at 8:19; be- with lands and nations "in". The four phrases keep the order and the small words that the Hebrew gives them each time (10:5, 20, 31, 32): 10:31 ends legoyehem "according to their nations", where 10:20 has begoyehem "in their nations". goy "nation" (Job 12:23); mishpachah "family" (8:19); erets in the plural "lands"; toledot (10:1, 32) "generations", with no bracket.
- i "coastland": the word covers coasts and islands. iyye haggoyim (10:5; Zeph 2:11) "the coastland nations": the lands stand for the peoples who live in them, as kol-ha'arets does at 9:19, and 10:32 has "the nations" in the same place.
- hu at the head of a note on a person or a place (10:8, 9, 12; 2:11; 4:20-21) only names the subject and is not marked: "He began", "He was", "that is". hechel lihyot gibbor "began to be a mighty man", as planned. gibbor tsayid "a mighty hunter", keeping "mighty" (gibbor is spelled two ways in 10:9, and the English is the same), with tsayid beside tsud "hunt" (Job 10:16); lifne YHWH "before Yahweh", as lifne ha'elohim is "before God" (6:11). al-ken ye'amar "That is why it is said"; the saying stands in quotation marks. reshit "beginning" and mamlakhah "kingdom" (the same English as arche and basileia). yatsa "go out" (10:11) or "come out" (10:14), by where the writer stands (8:19; Job 1:21). ba'arets (10:8, 32) "on the earth", as at 6:4; ha'arets hahi "that land".
- bekhor "firstborn" (Job 1:13); achar (10:18) "afterward" (Job 18:2). gevul "border". bo'akhah (10:19, 30; 13:10; 25:18) "as you go toward": the Hebrew is "your going", and the ending on the place name is "toward"; ad "as far as". The two halves of 10:19 have no "and" between them and stand on either side of a semicolon. moshav (10:30) "the place where they lived", beside yashav "live" (4:16; 11:2). har with the name of a region (10:30; 31:21; 36:8) "the hill country of"; elsewhere it stays "mountain". qedem "east" (2:8).
- haggadol of a brother or a son: of one among three "the eldest" (10:21), as haqqatan is "the youngest" (9:24); of one of two "the elder" or "the older" (27:1, 15). avi kol-bene-Ever "the father of all the sons of Eber": "sons of" is kept, as it is all through the chapter. (Eber is the name heard in "Hebrew", 14:13; nothing is said of it here, and that chapter may want a note.)
- For Genesis 11: safah (11:1, 6, 7, 9) is not lashon "language" (10:5, 20, 31), and "speech" (Job 12:20) would keep the two apart; kol-ha'arets (11:1, 9) is the people of the earth, as at 9:19. puts (11:4, 8, 9) "scatter", "be scattered", as at 9:19 and 10:18. erets Shin'ar (11:2) "the land of Shinar" and Bavel (11:9) "Babylon", as at 10:10, with a note that the name sounds like the word for "confused"; miqqedem beside qedem "east" (10:30; 2:8); yashav "live", banah "build" and ir "city" (10:11-12); hechel "begin" (10:8; 9:20). 11:10 elleh toledot Shem "These are the generations of Shem", with no "And". The decision log of 10:21 quotes 11:10 ahead of time (Shem was "a hundred years old", as at 5:32, and fathered Arpachshad "two years after the flood"), and that of 10:24 quotes 11:12 in the wording of chapter 5 ("And Arpachshad lived thirty-five years, and he fathered Shelah"); yalad in chapter 10 and holid in chapter 11 are both "fathered" (the Genesis 5 line). Arpachshad, Shelah, Eber and Peleg as at 10:22-25, and the note on "Peleg" (10:25) is not repeated. The alt about Kenan stands at 10:24; at 11:12-13 the old Greek has him again, with his years, and it is the same alternate. Kasdim (11:28, 31) "Chaldeans" (Job 1:17); erets Kena'an "the land of Canaan".

## Settled in Genesis 11
- safah of what people speak (11:1, 6, 7, 9) "speech", kept distinct from lashon "language" (10:5, 20, 31).
- devarim achadim (11:1) "the same words" [alt: few words] (decision log, call: clear).
- nasa (11:2; 13:11) "travel". miqqedem with it "eastward" [alt at 11:2: from the east] (decision log, call: close); of where a thing lies it stays "in the east", "east of" (2:8; 3:24).
- biq'ah (11:2) "plain".
- ish el-re'ehu (11:3) "to one another"; ish sefat re'ehu (11:7) "one another's speech".
- havah (11:3, 4, 7) "Come,".
- lavan levenim (11:3) "make bricks"; levenah "brick"; even "stone". saraf lisrefah of bricks "bake thoroughly" (saraf elsewhere "burn").
- chemar (11:3; 14:10; Exod 2:3) "tar", kept distinct from kofer "pitch" (6:14). chomer of building (11:3; Exod 1:14) "mortar" ("clay" at Job 4:19).
- migdal "tower"; rosh of a tower "top", as of a mountain (8:5); bashamayim (11:4) "in the sky".
- asah shem (11:4) "make a name". pen (11:4) "so that ... not", as listed. puts with al-pene khol-ha'arets (11:4, 8, 9) "scatter over the face of the whole earth".
- yarad of Yahweh (11:5, 7) "come down", "go down". bene ha'adam (11:5) "the sons of humankind", as benot ha'adam is "the daughters of humankind" (6:2).
- zamam (11:6) "intend", kept distinct from chashav "plan". nivtsar min (11:6; Job 42:2) "be out of the reach of".
- balal (11:7, 9) "confuse", with one bracketed note at 11:9 that it sounds like the name "Babylon". shama of a speech (11:7; 42:23) "understand".
- chadal with an infinitive (11:8) "stop".
- al-ken qara shemah (11:9) "That is why it was named": qara with no subject named is an English passive.
- 11:10-26 keeps the genealogy formula of chapter 5, without its third sentence; we-X chay (11:12, 14) is the same English as wayechi X, "And X lived".
- al-pene of a person (11:28) "in the presence of". erets moledet (11:28; 24:7; 31:13) "the land of one's birth".
- aqarah (11:30) "barren": it shares its English with galmud (Job 3:7). walad "child". kallah "daughter-in-law". ben-beno "his son's son".
- wayyeshvu sham (11:2, 31) "and they lived there", yashav as listed. ad with a place (11:31) "as far as", as at 10:19. lalekhet artsah Kena'an (11:31; 12:5) "to go to the land of Canaan".
- Names: Reu, Serug, Nahor, Terah, Abram, Lot, Sarai, Milcah, Iscah; Ur Kasdim "Ur of the Chaldeans". Haran (11:26-31), Terah's son, and Charan (11:31, 32; 12:4, 5), the place, are both "Haran", as English has them, with one bracketed note at 11:31 that they are two names.

## Settled in Genesis 12
- lekh-lekha (12:1; 22:2) "Go": the lekha is not shown, as at Deut 5:30.
- moledet by itself (12:1; 24:4; 43:7) "relatives" [alt at 12:1: your birthplace] (decision log, call: clear); erets moledet stays "the land of one's birth" (11:28). bet av "father's house".
- asah le-goy (12:2) "make into a nation"; goy gadol "a great nation". giddel shem "make a name great". berakhah "blessing"; wehyeh berakhah "and be a blessing" [alt: so that you will be a blessing] (decision log, call: close).
- qillel of one person against another (12:3) "call down a curse on", beside arar "curse" in the same sentence; of Yahweh it stays "bring a curse on" (8:21).
- nivrekhu be- (12:3; 18:18; 28:14) "be blessed in" [alt at 12:3: bless themselves by] (decision log, call: clear). mishpechot ha'adamah "the families of the ground", adamah as listed.
- rekush "possessions"; rakhash "amass". asah of people or wealth (12:5; 31:1) "gain". hannefesh of a household's people "the people".
- avar ba- (12:6) "pass through". meqom Shekhem "the place of Shechem". elon (12:6; 13:18; 14:13; 18:1) "oak": it shares its English with allon (35:8), which is written with the same letters.
- hakkena'ani az ba'arets (12:6) "the Canaanites were then in the land", as open as the Hebrew (already there, or still there); 13:7 has the same words with yoshev "living".
- nir'ah el of Yahweh (12:7) "appear to". wayyiven sham mizbeach laYHWH (12:7, 8; 13:18) "built an altar there to Yahweh".
- he'etiq with no object (12:8; 26:22) "move on". natah ohel "pitch a tent" (natah elsewhere "stretch out"). haharah "to the hill country". miyyam "on the west"; miqqedem "on the east", "east of".
- halokh wenasoa after nasa (12:9) "travel steadily on", halokh as at 8:3. hannegev "the Negev", the name of the region; hannegbah "toward the Negev".
- kaved of a famine (12:10; 41:31; 43:1) "severe". yarad Mitsraymah "go down to Egypt". gur "stay", as listed.
- hiqriv lavo (12:11) "come near to entering". hinneh-na "Look now"; na in a request to a person (12:13; 13:8) "please" (with an imperative in Job it is "now").
- yafeh "beautiful": it shares its English with tov of looks (6:2). yefat-mar'eh "beautiful in appearance".
- chiyyah of a person who is not killed (12:12) "let live". wechayetah nafshi (12:13; 19:20) "and I may live": nafshi is "I".
- hetiv le- (12:16) "treat well", beside yatav le- "go well with" (12:13). ba'avur "because of", as listed; biglal "on account of"; al-devar (12:17; 20:11, 18) "over the matter of".
- sar of a king's men (12:15) "official" ("prince" at Job 3:15). hillel "praise". bet par'oh "Pharaoh's house"; beto of his people (12:17) "his household", as at 7:1.
- tson-uvaqar (12:16; 13:5) "flocks and cattle" (miqneh stays "herds"). chamor "donkey" and aton "female donkey", as listed. eved and shifchah in a list of property (12:16) "male slaves and female slaves"; shifchah is kept distinct from amah "female servant".
- nigga with nega'im (12:17) "strike with plagues"; nega (Exod 11:1) "plague". It shares "strike" with shuf (3:15).
- qara le- (12:18) "call". laqach li le'ishah (12:19) "take as my wife". hinneh ishtekha "look, here is your wife". tsiwwah al "command concerning". shillach (12:20) "send out", as listed.
- Names: Shechem, Moreh, Bethel, Ai, the Negev, Pharaoh; hammitsrim "the Egyptians".

## Settled in Genesis 13
- kaved of wealth (13:2) "rich": kaved me'od "very rich" ("severe" of a famine, 12:10). kesef "silver" and zahav "gold", as listed.
- lemassa'av (13:3; Exod 17:1; Num 10:12) "by his stages": massa is the noun of nasa "travel" (11:2).
- battechillah (13:3) "at the beginning" (it shares its English with reshit); barishonah (13:4) "at the first".
- wayyiqra sham Avram beshem YHWH (13:4) "and there Abram called on the name of Yahweh" [alt: where Abram had called on the name of Yahweh] (decision log, call: clear).
- nasa of a land and those who live on it (13:6; 36:7) "support"; nasa enayim (13:10, 14) "lift one's eyes", as listed. yachdav "together", as yachad. yakhol "be able".
- riv of people in a story (13:7; 26:20-22) "quarrel" ("contend" stays in the speeches of Job); merivah (13:8) "quarreling".
- ro'e miqneh (13:7) "the shepherds of ... herds", ro'eh and miqneh as listed.
- anashim achim (13:8) "men who are brothers"; ach stays "brother" where it is a brother's son (13:8, 11; 14:14, 16). ish me'al achiv (13:11) "each from his brother".
- nifrad me'al, me'im (13:9, 11, 14) "separate from", parad as listed.
- hassemol, hayyamin (13:9) "the left", "the right"; hemin "go to the right"; hismil "go to the left".
- kikkar (13:10, 11, 12; 19:17, 25, 28, 29) "plain": kikkar hayyarden "the plain of the Jordan"; are hakkikkar "the cities of the plain". It shares its English with biq'ah (11:2); Deut 34:3 has the two side by side.
- mashqeh (13:10) "well watered", beside hishqah "water" (2:6, 10). gan-YHWH "the garden of Yahweh". lifne with an infinitive "before".
- shichet of Sodom and Gomorrah (13:10; 19:13, 14, 29) "ruin", as listed.
- bachar lo (13:11) "choose for himself".
- ahal (13:12, 18) "move one's tent", kept distinct from natah ohel "pitch a tent" (12:8).
- anshe Sedom (13:13; 19:4) "the men of Sodom". chatta "sinner" (the same English as hamartolos): chatta'im laYHWH "sinners against Yahweh". me'od at the end of a sentence with no verb (13:13) "very much so".
- na with an imperative, of Yahweh to a person (13:14) "now".
- tsafonah, negbah, qedmah, yammah (13:14; 28:14) "northward", "southward", "eastward", "westward".
- ad-olam (13:15) "forever": it shares its English with le'olam.
- sim ke- (13:16) "make like". afar ha'arets (13:16; 28:14) "the dust of the earth". manah "count".
- le'orkah ulerochbah (13:17) "through its length and its width", orekh and rochav as listed.
- be'elone Mamre (13:18; 14:13; 18:1) "among the oaks of Mamre".
- Names: the Perizzites, the Jordan, Zoar, Mamre, Hebron.

## Settled in Genesis 14
- melekh goyim (14:1, 9; Josh 12:23) "king of Goiim" [alt at 14:1: king of nations] (decision log, call: close).
- asah milchamah et (14:2) "make war against"; arakh milchamah et (14:8) "draw up for battle against": milchamah "war" or "battle", by context. et and ittam of the side fought (14:2, 8, 9) "against".
- hu / hi before the other name of a place (14:2, 3, 7, 8, 17) "(that is, ...)", in parentheses.
- chavar el (14:3) "join together at".
- emeq (14:3, 8, 10, 17) "valley", kept distinct from kikkar and biq'ah "plain". yam hammelach "the Salt Sea". emeq hammelekh (14:17; 2 Sam 18:18) "the King's Valley".
- avad with a king as its object (14:4) "serve", as listed. marad "rebel" (pesha stays "rebellion").
- ushelosh-esreh shanah maradu (14:4) "and in the thirteenth year they rebelled" [alt: and for thirteen years they had rebelled] (decision log, call: close).
- hikkah of peoples and kings in war (14:5, 7, 15, 17) "strike down", as listed.
- beharram Se'ir (14:6) "in their hill country, Seir", har as listed. al-hammidbar "by the wilderness".
- shuv on a march (14:7) "turn back". sedeh with the name of a people (14:7; 32:3) "the country of" (sadeh stays "field").
- be'erot be'erot chemar (14:10) "full of tar pits": a noun said twice is "full of"; be'er elsewhere (21:25; 26:15) "well". nus "flee": it shares its English with barach. wayyippelu shammah "and they fell there".
- palit (14:13; Ezek 24:26) "one who had escaped", beside nimlat "escape".
- ha'ivri (14:13; 39:14) "the Hebrew". ba'ale berit (14:13) "have a covenant with": berit stays "covenant".
- nishbah (14:14) "be taken captive". heriq of men (14:14) "lead out". chanikh (only here) "trained man". yelid bayit (14:14; 17:12, 13, 23, 27) "born in the house". radaf with no object "go in pursuit".
- nechelaq al (14:15) "divide one's forces against". missemol le- (14:15) "north of" (Ezek 16:46). eved of the men of Abram's house (14:15) "servant".
- heshiv with possessions and people (14:16) "bring back". yatsa liqrat (14:17) "go out to meet".
- El Elyon (14:18, 19, 20, 22) "God Most High". kohen le- (14:18) "a priest of".
- barukh with le- of the one who blesses (14:19; Ps 115:15) "Blessed is ... by".
- qoneh shamayim wa'arets (14:19, 22) "owner of heaven and earth" [alt at 14:19: who brought heaven and earth into being] (decision log, call: ask). shamayim wa'arets with no article "heaven and earth".
- miggen beyad (14:20) "deliver into the hand of"; tsar "foe", as listed. ma'aser "a tenth".
- herim yad el (14:22) "raise one's hand to": the gesture of the oath is kept. im in an oath (14:23) "not", as listed. chut "thread"; serokh na'al "sandal strap". he'eshir "make rich".
- bil'aday (14:24) "Nothing for me". cheleq of what was taken in war (14:24) "share" ("portion" stays for what God allots).
- Names: Amraphel, Arioch, Ellasar, Chedorlaomer, Tidal, Goiim; Bera, Birsha, Shinab, Shemeber, Bela; Siddim; the Rephaim, the Zuzim, the Emim (Deut 2:10-11), the Horites, the Amalekites; Ashteroth-Karnaim, Ham (not the name of Noah's son), Shaveh-Kiriathaim, Seir, El-Paran, En-Mishpat, Kadesh, Hazazon-Tamar; Eshcol, Aner; Dan, Hobah, Damascus; Shaveh; Melchizedek, Salem.

## Settled in Genesis 15
- achar haddevarim ha'elleh (15:1; 22:1) "After these things". hayah devar-YHWH el (15:1) "the word of Yahweh came to"; with no verb (15:4) the same. machazeh "vision": it shares its English with chizzayon.
- magen "shield", as listed; anokhi magen lakh "I am a shield to you". sakhar (15:1) "reward": sekharkha harbeh me'od "your reward will be very great" [alt: I am a shield to you, your very great reward] (decision log, call: close).
- Adonai YHWH (15:2, 8) "Lord Yahweh".
- ariri "childless"; holekh ariri "go childless".
- ben-mesheq beti (15:2; mesheq only here) "the one who will get my house" [alt: the one in charge of my house] (decision log, call: close).
- ben-bayit (15:3) "one of my household", kept distinct from yelid bayit "born in the house" (14:14).
- yarash with a person as its object (15:3, 4) "be the heir of" ("be my heir", "be your heir"); of land it stays "take possession of" (15:7, 8).
- me'im of a father (15:4; 2 Sam 7:12) "body" ("stomach" at Job 20:14). ki-im after a negative (15:4) "rather".
- hibbit (15:5) "look": it shares its English with hinneh. safar "count" and kokhav "star", as listed.
- he'emin be- (15:6) "put one's trust in", as listed. chashav with le- of a person (15:6) "count to ... as". tsedaqah "righteousness": it shares its English with tsedeq.
- bammah (15:8) "how".
- eglah "heifer"; ez "female goat"; ayil "ram"; tor "turtledove"; gozal (15:9; Deut 32:11) "young bird". meshullash of an animal (15:9) "three-year-old".
- batar (15:10) "cut in two"; beter "half"; gezarim (15:17) "pieces". liqrat of things set facing each other "opposite".
- ayit "birds of prey". peger "carcass". hishshiv (15:11) "drive away".
- hashemesh lavo (15:12) "the sun was about to set"; hashemesh ba'ah (15:17) "the sun had set".
- chashekhah (15:12) "darkness", as choshekh. alatah (15:17; Ezek 12:6, 7, 12) "dark".
- yadoa teda (15:13) "Know for certain".
- innah (15:13; 16:6) "afflict", beside oni "affliction".
- dan (15:14) "judge": it shares its English with shafat.
- bo el-avot (15:15) "go to one's fathers". niqbar "be buried". sevah tovah (15:15; 25:8) "a good old age".
- dor revi'i (15:16) "in the fourth generation". shalem "complete"; ad-hennah "yet".
- tannur "oven"; tannur ashan "a smoking oven". lappid esh "a flaming torch".
- natatti in the words of a covenant (15:18) "I give", as at 9:3.
- nehar Mitsrayim (15:18) "the river of Egypt". hannahar haggadol nehar-Perat "the great river, the river Euphrates".
- Names: Eliezer; the Kenites, the Kenizzites, the Kadmonites, the Hittites.

## Settled in Genesis 16
- gevirah (16:4, 8, 9) "mistress". shifchah of Hagar "female slave", as listed.
- atsar with min (16:2; 20:18) "hold back from", as atsar be-.
- nivnah min of a woman (16:2; 30:3) "be built up from", with one bracketed note at 16:2 that it sounds like "son".
- shama leqol (16:2; 3:17) "listen to the voice of"; shama el (16:11) "hear".
- natan ... lo le'ishah (16:3) "give ... as his wife".
- qalal be'ene of a person (16:4, 5) "count for little in the eyes of".
- chamasi alekha (16:5) "The violence done to me is on you". natan becheq "give into the arms of".
- shafat ben ... uven (16:5) "judge between ... and"; the jussive "May".
- hattov be'ene (16:6) "what is good in the eyes of". barach mippene (16:6, 8) "flee from".
- mal'akh YHWH "the angel of Yahweh". ayin "spring": it shares its English with ma'yan. bederekh with a place (16:7) "on the way to".
- e-mizzeh va't (16:8) "from where have you come", as at Job 2:2.
- hit'annah (16:9) "let oneself be afflicted" [alt: humble oneself] (decision log, call: close), beside innah "afflict" and oni "affliction".
- lo yissafer merov (16:10; 32:12) "be too many to count".
- harah, the adjective (16:11) "pregnant".
- pere adam (16:12) "a wild donkey of a man". yad be- "hand against".
- al-pene khol-echav (16:12; 25:18) "in the presence of all his brothers" [alt at 16:12: in defiance of all his brothers] (decision log, call: close).
- El ro'i (16:13) "a God of seeing". ra'iti acharei ro'i "seen the back of the one who sees me" [alt: gone on seeing, after the one who sees me] (decision log, call: ask).
- Names: Hagar, Ishmael, Shur, Bered; Be'er Lachay Ro'i (16:14; 24:62; 25:11) "Beer-Lahai-Roi".

## Settled in Genesis 17
- El Shadday (17:1; 28:3; 35:11) "God Almighty": Shadday as at Job 5:17, with no bracket.
- hithallekh lifne (17:1; 24:40; 48:15) "walk before".
- natan berit ben (17:2) "set a covenant between", natan as at 9:12.
- bime'od me'od (17:2, 6, 20) "very greatly", as me'od me'od (7:19).
- nafal al-panav (17:3, 17) "fall on one's face". dibber et (17:3, 22, 23) "speak with", beside dibber el "speak to".
- hamon (17:4, 5) "multitude": av hamon goyim "the father of a multitude of nations". It shares its English with rov (Job 11:2).
- Avraham "Abraham" from 17:5 on, with one bracketed note at 17:5. Sarah "Sarah" from 17:15 on, with one bracketed note there. Yitschaq "Isaac", with one bracketed note at 17:19.
- hifrah (the hiphil of parah; 17:6, 20) "make fruitful"; hirbah "multiply", as ravah.
- natan with two objects (17:5) "make"; natan le- (17:6, 20) "make into", the same English as asah le- (12:2).
- ledorotam, ledorotekhem (17:7, 9, 12) "through their generations", "through your generations".
- hayah le- ... lelohim (17:7, 8) "be God to".
- erets megurim (17:8; 28:4; 36:7; 37:1) "the land where ... is staying", megurim and gur as listed.
- achuzzah (17:8; 23:4; 48:4) "holding": achuzzat olam "an everlasting holding". Kept distinct from rekush "possessions" and yarash "take possession of".
- shamar berit (17:9, 10) "keep a covenant".
- mul "circumcise"; the niphal "be circumcised", and with et-besar orlah "be circumcised in the flesh of the foreskin". orlah "foreskin"; arel "uncircumcised".
- ben-shemonat yamim (17:12) "at eight days old".
- miqnat kesef (17:12, 13, 23, 27) "bought with silver". ben-nekhar (17:12, 27) "foreigner": it shares its English with nokhri.
- hannefesh hahi (17:14) "that person". nikhrat me'ammav "be cut off from one's people", nikhrat as listed. hefer berit "break a covenant".
- 17:16 uverakhtiha wehayetah ... mimmennah "I will bless her, and she will become ... from her" [alt: him, he, from him, as the old Greek and Latin versions read] (decision log, call: clear). malkhe ammim "kings of peoples".
- tsachaq (17:17; 18:12) "laugh": it shares its English with sachaq.
- aval (17:19; 42:21) "Indeed" [alt at 17:19: But] (decision log, call: close); omnam stays "Truly".
- nasi (17:20; 25:16) "chieftain", kept distinct from sar "prince", "official" and rosh "chief", "head".
- lammo'ed hazzeh (17:21) "at this set time", mo'ed as at 1:14; bashanah ha'acheret "next year".
- alah me'al (17:22; 35:13) "go up from".

## Settled in Genesis 18
- nitsav al (18:2) "stand near"; amad al (18:8) "stand by"; amad lifne (18:22) "stand before". ruts liqrat "run to meet". hishtachawah artsah "bow down to the ground".
- Adonay in Abraham's mouth (18:3) "Lord" [alt: My lord] (decision log, call: ask); with a preposition (18:27, 30, 31, 32) "the Lord".
- im-na (18:3) "if": its na is carried by the "please" of the request.
- avar me'al (18:3) "pass ... by"; avar alone (18:5) "pass on"; avar al "pass ... way". ki-al-ken (18:5; 19:8) "since".
- laqach of water and food for guests (18:4, 5) "fetch". nish'an with no al (18:4) "recline". pat-lechem "a piece of bread".
- sa'ad lev (18:5; Judg 19:5) "strengthen oneself": the idiom is translated by its meaning.
- mihar (18:6, 7) "hurry". se'ah "seah": the unit is kept, as ammah is. qemach "flour"; solet "fine flour". lush "knead". uggah "cake".
- ben-baqar (18:7, 8) "calf", baqar as listed. rakh "tender". hanna'ar (18:7) "the young man". asah of food "prepare". natan lifne "set before". chem'ah "curds" and chalav "milk", as listed.
- hinneh in answer to "Where?" (18:9) "There,".
- ka'et chayyah (18:10, 14; 2 Kgs 4:16, 17) "about this time next year", as ka'et machar is "about this time tomorrow" (Exod 9:18). lammo'ed (18:14) "At the set time".
- zaqen as an adjective (18:11, 12) "old"; zaqan (18:13) "grow old". ba bayyamim (18:11; 24:1) "advanced in years".
- orach kannashim (18:11) "the way of women".
- beqirbah (18:12) "within herself", (18:24) "within it"; betokh ha'ir (18:24, 26) "inside the city".
- balah of a person (18:12) "be worn out". ednah (only here) "pleasure". adoni of a husband "my lord".
- ha'af (18:13, 23, 24) "really"; umnam "truly", as omnam: ha'af umnam "really and truly". lammah zeh "Why is it that".
- nifla min (18:14) "be too hard for"; the echo with nifla'ot "wonders" is not shown.
- kichesh with no object (18:15) "deny it".
- hishqif al-pene (18:16; 19:28) "look down toward". shillach of guests (18:16) "send on one's way".
- kissah min (18:17) "hide from": a fifth root that shares "hide".
- atsum (18:18) "powerful", leaving "mighty" for gibbor. goye ha'arets "the nations of the earth".
- yada of Yahweh and Abraham (18:19) "know": yeda'tiv "I have known him". lema'an asher "so that ... may". hevi al "bring on"; dibber al "speak concerning".
- shamar derekh YHWH (18:19) "keep the way of Yahweh". asah tsedaqah umishpat "do righteousness and justice"; asah mishpat (18:25) "do justice".
- za'aqah, tse'aqah of Sodom (18:20, 21; 19:13) "outcry" ("cry" at Job 16:18). kaved of sin (18:20) "heavy".
- asu kalah (18:21) "have done altogether" [alt: have done ...; if so, it is the end] (decision log, call: clear).
- panah (18:22) "turn". nagash (18:23) "approach".
- safah (18:23, 24; 19:15, 17) "sweep away".
- nasa le- of a place or a people (18:24, 26) "forgive". lema'an with a noun (18:24) "for the sake of"; ba'avur stays "because of" (18:26, 29, 31, 32).
- chalilah lekha (18:25) "Far be it from you". shofet kol-ha'arets "the judge of the whole earth".
- ho'il with an infinitive (18:27, 31) "take it on oneself". afar wa'efer "dust and ashes".
- chaser with an object (18:28) "lack", as planned. be- of what a thing is done for (18:28) "over". shichet stays "ruin" (18:28, 31, 32).
- akh happa'am (18:32) "only this once". halakh of Yahweh leaving (18:33) "go away".

## Settled in Genesis 19
- yada of sex outside marriage too (19:5, 8) "be intimate with", as Obay's line on 4:1.
- hishtachawah appayim artsah (19:1) "bow down with one's face to the ground". sha'ar "gate". rechov "square".
- patsar be- with me'od (19:3, 9) "press ... hard"; uts be- (19:15) "urge".
- matsot "unleavened bread"; afah "bake".
- miqqatseh of a whole people (19:4) "to the last one". minna'ar we'ad-zaqen "from young to old"; miqqaton we'ad-gadol (19:11) "from the smallest to the greatest".
- hera (19:7, 9) "do evil"; hera le- ... min "do worse evil to ... than to".
- katov be'ene (19:8) "as is good in the eyes of". betsel qorati "under the shadow of my roof".
- gesh-hal'ah (19:9) "Stand back!" gur with no place named (19:9) "stay as a foreigner". A verb followed by its own infinitive absolute (19:9 wayyishpot shafot) "actually".
- shavar "break". sanwerim (19:11; 2 Kgs 6:18) "blindness": hikkah ba- "strike with". la'ah with an infinitive (19:11) "weary oneself trying to".
- chatan "son-in-law". loqeche venotav (19:14) "who were to marry his daughters" [alt: who had married his daughters] (decision log, call: close).
- A participle of what is about to happen, with no hinneh (19:13, 14) "be going to", as at 7:4.
- tsicheq (the piel; 19:14; 21:9; 26:8; 39:14) "joke" where it fits, beside tsachaq "laugh".
- hannimtsa'ot (19:15) "who are here". avon of a city about to be ruined (19:15) "punishment".
- hitmahmah "linger". hecheziq beyad (19:16) "take hold of the hand of". chemlah (19:16; Isa 63:9), the noun of chamal "spare": bechemlat YHWH alav "because Yahweh was sparing him".
- hinniach of a person (19:16) "set down" ("settle" at 2:15).
- nimlat al-nefesh (19:17) "escape for one's life". hibbit achare "look behind"; amad of one who is fleeing "stop". haharah, bahar (19:17, 19, 30) "to the hill country", "in the hill country", as at 12:8 and 14:10.
- Adonay in Lot's mouth (19:18) "Lord" [alt: my lords] (decision log, call: ask); adonay to the two (19:2) "my lords".
- higdil chesed (19:19) "make one's faithful love great"; asah chesed im "show faithful love to". hecheyah et-nefesh "keep alive": nafshi is "me", as at 12:13.
- davaq of disaster, with a person as its object (19:19) "overtake". hara'ah (19:19) "the disaster", as at Job 2:11.
- mits'ar (19:20) "a small thing", as at Job 8:7, with one bracketed note at 19:22 that the name "Zoar" sounds like it.
- nasa panim le- of a request (19:21) "grant": the idiom is translated by its meaning (of taking sides it stays "show favoritism to").
- hafakh of cities (19:21, 25, 29) "overturn", as listed; hafekhah (19:29) "the overturning" (mahpekhah, Deut 29:23, is to stand beside it).
- yatsa of the sun (19:23) "rise". himtir with an object (19:24) "rain". gofrit wa'esh "sulfur and fire". min-hashamayim "from the sky".
- tsemach ha'adamah (19:25) "what sprouted from the ground". netsiv melach "a pillar of salt".
- hishkim ... el (19:27) "rise early ... and go to", with "go" supplied.
- qitor (19:28) "smoke": it shares its English with ashan. kivshan "furnace", kept distinct from tannur "oven".
- mittokh (19:29) "from the midst of". me'arah "cave".
- bekhirah "the firstborn" (of two daughters), as bekhor; tse'irah "the younger".
- ba'arets (19:31) "in the land" [alt: on the earth] (decision log, call: close). kederekh kol-ha'arets "as is the way of all the earth".
- bo al of a man and a woman (19:31; Deut 25:5) "sleep with", as bo el (6:4). shakhav im and shakhav et (19:32-35) "lie with".
- hishqah yayin (19:32-35) "make ... drink wine". chiyyah zera min (19:32, 34) "keep offspring alive from". harah min (19:36) "conceive by".
- mimmochorat "on the next day"; emesh "last night"; ballaylah hu "that night".
- hu avi ... ad-hayyom (19:37, 38) "He is the father of ... to this day". bene Ammon "the sons of Ammon".
- Names: Moab, Ben-Ammi, Ammon.

## Settled in Genesis 20
- bachalom hallaylah (20:3; 31:24) "in a dream by night", chalom as listed.
- hinnekha met (20:3) "Look, you are about to die": hinneh with a participle, as at 6:13.
- be'ulat ba'al (20:3; Deut 22:22) "a married woman".
- qarav el of a man and a woman (20:4) "come near".
- tom levav (20:5, 6) "the blamelessness of one's heart", tom as at Job 4:6. niqyon kappayim "the innocence of one's palms", beside naqi "innocent" and kaf "palm".
- gam anokhi (20:6, twice) "I too". chasakh min (20:6) "restrain from", as at Job 7:11. natan with an infinitive (20:6; 31:7) "let".
- heshiv of a wife (20:7, 14) "give back".
- navi (20:7) "prophet". hitpallel be'ad (20:7) "pray for"; hitpallel el (20:17) "pray to".
- dibber be'ozne (20:8) "speak in the ears of", as at Deut 5:1.
- chata'ah (20:9) "sin": it shares its English with chatta't. ma'asim asher lo ye'asu "deeds that are not done".
- mah ra'ita ki (20:10) "What did you see, that".
- raq at the head of a saying (20:11) "surely". yir'at elohim "fear of God", as in Job.
- omnah (20:12; Josh 7:20) "truly", as omnam.
- hit'ah (20:13) "make wander", as at Job 12:24. elohim with a plural verb (20:13; 35:7) "God" [alt at 20:13: the gods] (decision log, call: clear).
- chesed between husband and wife (20:13) "loyalty": asah chesed im "show loyalty to".
- amar el and amar le- of the person spoken about (20:2, 13) "say of".
- elef kesef (20:16) "a thousand pieces of silver", with "pieces" supplied.
- kesut enayim (20:16) "a covering of the eyes": the picture is kept. hu before it "it is" [alt: he is] (decision log, call: clear).
- nokhachat (the niphal of hokhiach; 20:16) "be cleared" [alt: be rebuked] (decision log, call: ask).
- amah (20:17; 21:10) "female servant", as at Job 19:15, kept distinct from shifchah "female slave" (20:14).
- atsar be'ad rechem (20:18) "hold back a womb", atsar as at 16:2; the doubled verb "completely".
- Names: Abimelech.

## Settled in Genesis 21
- paqad of Yahweh and a person (21:1; 50:24) "attend to"; of what one owns it stays "inspect" (Job 5:24).
- zequnim (21:2, 7; 37:3) "old age": lizqunav "in his old age".
- millel with le- (21:7) "say to" ("utter" at Job 8:2). heniq "nurse", as yanaq. nigmal (21:8) "be weaned".
- tsechoq (21:6) "laughter". tsachaq le- (21:6) "laugh with" [alt: laugh at] (decision log, call: close).
- metsacheq with nothing after it (21:9) "laughing" [alt: mocking] (decision log, call: ask); "joke" stays at 19:14.
- yarash im (21:10) "be heir with".
- ra be'ene (21:11, 12) "be bad in the eyes of", beside tov be'ene "good in the eyes of".
- al-odot (21:11) "on account of"; (21:25) "over".
- yeled (21:8, 14, 15, 16) "child"; na'ar of Ishmael (21:12, 17-20) "boy".
- beYitschaq yiqqare lekha zara (21:12) "in Isaac offspring will be called yours".
- sim le-goy (21:13, 18) "make into a nation", as asah le-goy (12:2).
- chemet (21:14, 15, 19) "skin": chemat mayim "a skin of water".
- shillach of Hagar (21:14) "send out", beside garash "drive out" (21:10), the pair of 3:23-24.
- ta'ah (21:14; 37:15) "wander", beside hit'ah "make wander" (20:13).
- kalah of water (21:15) "run out". hishlikh of a person (21:15; 37:22) "throw". siach "shrub", as at 2:5.
- minneged (21:16) "opposite"; harcheq "far off"; kimtachawe qeshet "about a bowshot away". ra'ah be- "look on".
- mal'akh Elohim (21:17) "the angel of God". min-hashamayim of a call from God (21:17; 22:11, 15) "from the heavens".
- mah-lakh (21:17) "What is the matter?"
- hecheziq yad be- (21:18) "hold fast with one's hand". hishqah of a person (21:19) "give a drink".
- roveh qashshat (21:20) "an archer, a bowman".
- sar tsava (21:22, 32; 26:26) "commander of the army".
- nishba le- ... be- (21:23) "swear to ... by". shaqar le- "deal falsely with". chesed between Abimelech and Abraham "loyalty", as at 20:13.
- hokhiach (21:25) "rebuke". gam ... gam with a negative (21:26) "neither ... nor"; bilti hayyom "except today".
- kivsah (21:28, 29, 30) "ewe lamb". hitsiv levad "set by themselves". edah (21:30; 31:52) "witness": it shares its English with ed.
- eshel (21:33) "tamarisk". El Olam "the Everlasting God".
- Names: Phicol; Be'er Shava "Beersheba"; midbar Paran "the wilderness of Paran".

## Settled in Genesis 22
- nissah (22:1) "test": it shares its English with dokimazo.
- hinneni in answer to a call (22:1, 7, 11) "Here I am".
- yachid (22:2, 12, 16) "only one": et-binkha et-yechidkha "your son, your only one".
- he'elah le'olah (22:2, 13) "offer up as a burnt offering", as he'elah olot (8:20).
- amar le- of a place (22:2, 3, 9) "tell ... of".
- chavash of a donkey (22:3) "saddle" ("bind up" stays for wounds). biqqa etsim "split wood".
- ne'arav (22:3, 5, 19) "his young men", as at 18:7; hanna'ar of Isaac (22:5, 12) "the boy", as of Ishmael (21:12).
- yashav with poh (22:5) "stay": it shares its English with gur. ad-koh "over there". hishtachawah with nothing after it (22:5) stays "bow down".
- ma'akhelet (22:6, 10) "knife".
- seh (22:7, 8) "sheep": one animal of the flock, of any age (Exod 12:5), as tson with a number is "sheep" (Job 1:3); "lamb" stays for keves and amnos.
- ra'ah lo (22:8) "see to ... for oneself". YHWH yir'eh (22:14) "Yahweh Will See to It"; yera'eh there "it will be seen to" [alt: he will be seen] (decision log, call: ask), with one bracketed note that the word is "see".
- arakh of wood (22:9) "arrange". aqad (only here) "bind": it shares its English with asar. mimma'al le- "on top of".
- shachat, the verb (22:10) "slaughter", the same English as sphazo.
- shalach yad el (22:12) "reach out one's hand against", as at Job 1:12. me'umah "anything".
- chasakh ... min of a thing not given (22:12, 16; 39:9) "withhold from": it shares its English with kala; of a person held from an act it stays "restrain" (20:6).
- achar by itself, of place (22:13) "behind him" [alt: one ram, as other copies have it] (decision log, call: clear). sevakh "thicket". tachat of one put for another (22:13) "in place of", as at 4:25.
- asher ye'amer hayyom (22:14) "as it is said today". har YHWH "the mountain of Yahweh".
- bi nishba'ti (22:16) "By myself I have sworn". ne'um YHWH "declares Yahweh". ya'an asher (22:16) and eqev asher (22:18; 26:5) both "because".
- kokhve hashamayim (22:17; 26:4) "the stars of the sky", as at 15:5. sefat hayyam "the shore of the sea".
- yarash sha'ar (22:17; 24:60) "take possession of the gate of". oyevav of the offspring (22:17) "their enemies" [alt: his enemies] (decision log, call: clear).
- hitbarekhu be- (22:18; 26:4) "be blessed in", the same English as nivrekhu be- (12:3) [alt at 22:18: bless themselves by] (decision log, call: close). shama beqol (22:18) "listen to the voice of", as at 21:12.
- wayyuggad le- ... lemor (22:20) "it was told to ..., saying".
- pilegesh (22:24) "concubine".
- Names: Moriah; Buz, Kemuel, Chesed, Hazo, Pildash, Jidlaph, Bethuel, Rebekah, Reumah, Tebah, Gaham, Tahash, Maacah. Uz (22:21) is spelled as at 10:23 and Job 1:1.

## Settled in Genesis 23
- chayye X (23:1; 25:7, 17) "the life of X"; shene chayye "the years of the life of".
- safad le- (23:2) "lament for", kept distinct from aval "mourn". bakah with a person as its object "weep for".
- met of one who is to be buried (23:3-15) "dead": "his dead", "my dead", "your dead". me'al pene meto (23:3) "from beside his dead". qavar "bury"; qavar millefanay (23:4, 8) "bury out of my sight".
- toshav (23:4; Lev 25:23; Ps 39:12) "settler": ger wetoshav "a resident foreigner and a settler".
- achuzzat-qever (23:4, 9, 20; 49:30; 50:13) "a holding for a grave".
- shama with a suffix or el, of heeding a person (23:6, 8, 11, 13, 15, 16) "listen to", the same English all six times.
- nesi elohim (23:6) "a chieftain of God" [alt: a mighty chieftain] (decision log, call: clear).
- mivchar (23:6) "the choicest of", beside bachar "choose". kala min (23:6) "withhold from", as planned.
- am ha'arets (23:7, 12, 13) "the people of the land".
- yesh et-nafshekhem (23:8; 2 Kgs 9:15) "you are willing": the idiom is translated by its meaning.
- paga be- ... le- (23:8) "appeal to ... for", as at Job 21:15.
- qetseh (23:9) "edge". bekhesef male (23:9; 1 Chr 21:22, 24) "for silver in full".
- yashav betokh of one who is present (23:10) "sit among". ba'e sha'ar iro (23:10, 18) "who entered the gate of his city".
- natatti of a gift or a payment made in the saying of it (23:11, 13) "I give", as at 9:3. le'ene (23:11, 18) "before the eyes of".
- lu before an imperative (23:13) "if only", as at Job 6:2.
- sheqel "shekel": the unit is kept, as ammah is. shaqal le- (23:16) "weigh out for". socher "merchant"; over lassocher "current among the merchants".
- qum le- of property (23:17, 20) "be made over to". miqnah (23:18) "purchase".
- lifne of a place (23:17) "in front of"; al-pene of a place (23:19; 25:9; 49:30; 50:13) "facing".
- ets, a collective (23:17) "trees". bekhol-gevulo saviv "within its whole border on every side".
- acharei-khen (23:19) "afterward", as at 6:4. qavar el "bury in".
- Names: Kiriath-Arba, Ephron, Zohar, Machpelah (the Hebrew has the article each time). bene Chet "the sons of Heth"; hachitti "the Hittite".

## Settled in Genesis 24
- eved of Abraham's servant (24:2-66) "servant", and adon "master" (the Job 3 line), also in the narrator's mouth (24:9, 10); adoni to a stranger (24:18) "my lord". avadim ushefachot in a list of property (24:35) "male slaves and female slaves", as at 12:16.
- zeqan beto (24:2) "the elder of his household". mashal be- of a steward (24:2) "govern", as listed.
- sim yad tachat yerekh (24:2, 9; 47:29) "put one's hand under the thigh of".
- hishbia (24:3, 37) "make swear". shevu'ah (24:8) "oath"; alah (24:41) "curse": the curse that an oath lays on the one who swears, kept distinct from shevu'ah. niqqah min and naqi min of an oath (24:8, 41; Josh 2:17, 20) "be clear of".
- elohe hashamayim (24:3, 7) "the God of the heavens"; welohe ha'arets "and the God of the earth".
- avah (24:5, 8) "be willing". halakh achare of a person (24:5, 8, 39, 61) "follow".
- heshiv of a person, to a place (24:5, 6, 8) "take back". hishamer lekha pen (24:6) "be careful that ... not".
- tuv (24:10; 45:18, 20) "good things".
- hivrikh of camels (24:11) "make kneel". sha'av "draw"; hasho'avot "the women who draw water", with "water" supplied.
- be'er (24:11, 20) "well" and ayin (24:13, 16, 29, 30, 42, 43, 45) "spring": never interchanged, as phrear and pege in John 4.
- hiqrah lifne (24:12; 27:20) "make it happen before".
- nitsav al of a spring (24:13, 43) "stand by": it shares its English with amad al (24:30).
- na'arah (24:14, 16, 28, 55, 57) "young woman", as na'ar is "young man"; na'aroteha (24:61) "her young women". almah (24:43) "girl", kept distinct. betulah (24:16) "virgin".
- kad "jar". hittah of a jar (24:14) "tilt"; horid (24:18, 46) "let down"; erah (24:20) "empty". shoqet (24:20; 30:38) "trough".
- hishqah of camels (24:14, 46) "water"; of a person (24:18, 19, 43, 45) "give a drink", as at 21:19. higmi (24:17; only here) "let sip".
- hokhiach le- (24:14, 44) "decide on ... for".
- tovat mar'eh (24:16; 26:7) "beautiful in appearance", the English of yefat-mar'eh (12:11).
- mishta'eh le- (24:21; only here) "gaze at" [alt: be astonished at] (decision log, call: clear). hecherish (24:21) "keep silent".
- hitsliach derekh (24:21, 40, 42, 56) "make one's way succeed".
- nezem "ring"; tsamid "bracelet"; yadayim of where bracelets are worn (24:22, 30, 47) "arms". beqa (24:22; Exod 38:26) "beka": the unit is kept, as sheqel is. asarah zahav (24:22) "ten shekels of gold", with "shekels" supplied.
- lin, lun (24:23, 25, 54) "spend the night", as at 19:2. mispo "fodder": it shares its English with belil.
- qadad (24:26, 48) "bow one's head", beside hishtachawah "bow down".
- emet "truth": chesed we'emet of God (24:27) "faithful love and truth", between people (24:49) "loyalty and truth"; derekh emet (24:48) "a true way". azav ... me'im (24:27) "abandon ... toward".
- nachah, hinchah (24:27, 48) "lead", as at Job 12:23.
- bet immah (24:28) "her mother's household".
- berukh YHWH (24:31; 26:29) "blessed of Yahweh". pinnah (24:31) "clear". pittach of camels (24:32) "unload".
- yesh with a suffix and a participle (24:42, 49) "be going to".
- im-lo after a prohibition (24:38) "Rather,".
- acharei ziqnatah (24:36) "after she had grown old".
- kelim (24:53) "articles". migdanot (24:53; Ezra 1:6) "precious things".
- shillach of a guest and of Rebekah (24:54, 56, 59) "send on one's way", as at 18:16. ichar (24:56) "delay".
- yamim o asor (24:55) "a few days, or ten" [alt: a year, or ten months] (decision log, call: clear).
- sha'al et-pi (24:57) "ask ... herself": the idiom is translated by its meaning.
- meneqet (24:59; 35:8) "nurse", beside heniq.
- alfe revavah (24:60) "thousands of ten thousands". sone'av of the offspring (24:60) "those who hate them", as "their enemies" at 22:17.
- rakhav al (24:61) "ride on".
- suach (24:63; only here) "meditate" [alt: walk about] (decision log, call: clear). lifnot erev "toward evening".
- nafal me'al of a rider (24:64; 2 Kgs 5:21) "drop down from".
- tsa'if (24:65; 38:14, 19) "veil". hitkassah "cover oneself".
- nicham, the niphal, of one who has lost someone (24:67; 38:12) "be comforted" ("regret" stays at 6:6-7).
- Names: Aram Naharayim "Aram-Naharaim"; Laban.

## Settled in Genesis 25
- hosif before a second verb (25:1) "again", as with an infinitive (8:10).
- mattanah (25:6) "gift". shillach me'al (25:6) "send away from". erets qedem "the land of the east".
- savea with nothing after it (25:8) "satisfied"; seva stays "full of".
- ne'esaf el-ammav (25:8, 17; 35:29; 49:33) "be gathered to his people".
- qanah of land (25:10) "buy", beside miqnah "purchase" (23:18).
- yashav im with a place (25:11) "live by".
- chatser (25:16) "village"; tirah "encampment". ummah (25:16; Num 25:15) "tribe": it shares its English with shevet.
- al-pene Mitsrayim (25:18) "facing Egypt", as al-pene of a place (23:19). nafal of where a people has its place (25:18; Judg 7:12) "settle".
- atar le- (25:21) "plead with": it shares its English with hitchannen. ne'tar le- "grant the plea of".
- lenokhach (25:21; 30:38) "in front of" [alt at 25:21: on behalf of] (decision log, call: close).
- hitrotsets (25:22; only here) "crush one another", ratsats as listed.
- lammah zeh anokhi (25:22) "why am I alive?" [alt: why is this happening to me?] (decision log, call: ask).
- darash et-YHWH of one who goes for an answer (25:22; 1 Kgs 22:8) "inquire of"; elsewhere darash stays "seek".
- beten of a mother (25:23, 24) "womb"; me'ayim of a mother "body", as of a father (15:4).
- le'om (25:23; 27:29) "people": it shares its English with am. amats min "be stronger than".
- rav ... tsa'ir (25:23) "the older", "the younger" [alt: the greater, the lesser] (decision log, call: close).
- male of days (25:24; 29:21) "be completed". te'omim "twins".
- admoni (25:25) "red", as adom (25:30). adderet "coat", kept distinct from simlah "cloak" and me'il "robe". se'ar "hair".
- qara shemo with no subject named (25:26, 30) "he was named", as at 11:9.
- achaz be- of a hand that keeps its hold (25:26) "hold on to".
- yodea tsayid (25:27) "who knew hunting"; tsayid of what is caught (25:28; 27:3) "game". ish tam (25:27) "a blameless man", tam as at Job 1:1.
- zid (25:29) "cook"; nazid "stew"; adashim "lentils". ayef "exhausted". hil'it (25:30; only here) "let gulp down".
- makhar "sell". bekhorah "birthright". kayyom (25:31, 33) "first".
- bazah (25:34) "despise", beside buz "contempt".
- Names: Keturah; Zimran, Jokshan, Medan, Midian, Ishbak, Shuah (Job 2:11 has "the Shuhite"); the Asshurim, the Letushim, the Leummim; Ephah, Epher, Abida, Eldaah; Nebaioth, Kedar, Adbeel, Mibsam, Mishma, Dumah, Massa, Hadad, Jetur, Naphish, Kedemah; Paddan-Aram; ha'arammi "the Aramean"; Esau, Jacob, Edom. Chanokh (25:4) "Enoch": the name of 4:17 and 5:18, spelled as it is there ("Hanoch" in many English Bibles; Obay can switch it).

## Settled in Genesis 26
- millevad (26:1) "besides". harishon of the earlier of two (26:1) "the first".
- shakan (26:2) "live" and gur (26:3) "stay", as listed. heqim et-hashevu'ah (26:3) "establish the oath", as heqim berit. ha'aratsot ha'el (26:3, 4) "these lands".
- mishmeret (26:5) "charge". chuqqah "statute", as choq. torah (26:5) "law", the same English as nomos.
- sha'al le- of a person (26:7) "ask about". al of a person (26:7, 9) "over". pen at the head of a thing said (26:7) "or", as at 19:19; (26:9) "So that ... not".
- arkhu-lo sham hayyamim (26:8) "he had been there a long time". hishqif be'ad hachallon "look down through the window".
- metsacheq et (26:8) "laughing with" [alt: caressing] (decision log, call: ask), with one bracketed note that it sounds like the name "Isaac".
- kim'at with a perfect (26:10) "could easily have". asham "guilt": it shares its English with avon.
- mot yumat (26:11) "will certainly be put to death".
- matsa of a crop (26:12) "get". me'ah she'arim "a hundredfold".
- gadal of a man and his wealth (26:13) "become great", "be great", as at 24:35; halokh wegadel "growing steadily greater".
- miqneh-tson, miqneh vaqar (26:14) "herds of sheep", "herds of cattle". avuddah "servants", as at Job 1:3. qinne with a person as its object (26:14) "envy", beside qin'ah "jealousy".
- sittem (26:15, 18) "stop up": it shares its English with niskar (8:2). afar of what fills a well (26:15) "earth".
- atsam min (26:16) "be more powerful than", beside atsum "powerful".
- nachal with the name of a place, and of where a well is dug (26:17, 19) "valley": it shares its English with emeq; "torrent" stays where it is the water (Job 6:15).
- shuv before a second verb (26:18) "again", as hosif (25:1).
- mayim chayyim (26:19) "living water", the same English as hydor zon (John 4:10), with no bracket.
- hit'asseq im (26:20; only here) "dispute with".
- karah of a well (26:25) "dig out", beside chafar "dig".
- merea (26:26) "friend", as at Job 6:14.
- shillach me'et (26:27) "send away from"; with beshalom (26:29) and of guests (26:31) "send on one's way".
- alah of what is sworn between two sides (26:28) "an oath under a curse"; it stays "curse" at 24:41. benotenu "between us".
- asah ra'ah im (26:29) "do harm to".
- ish le'achiv of men who are not kin (26:31) "to one another".
- morat ruach (26:35) "a bitterness of spirit".
- Names: Ahuzzath; Esek, Sitnah, Rehoboth (10:11 has Rehoboth-Ir), Shibah; Judith, Beeri, Basemath, Elon.

## Settled in Genesis 27
- kahah of eyes, with min and an infinitive (27:1) "grow too dim to", kahah as at Job 17:7.
- keli of a hunter (27:3) "gear"; teli (only here) "quiver". tsud tsayid (27:3, 5, 33) "hunt game"; tsayid of the hunting itself (27:30) "hunting".
- mat'ammim (27:4-31) "tasty food"; ka'asher ahavti, ahev "the kind I love", "he loves".
- nefesh as the one who blesses (27:4, 19, 25, 31) "soul": tevarekhekha nafshi "my soul may bless you", kept apart from the plain "bless" of 27:7, 10.
- ba'avur with an imperfect (27:4, 19, 31), ba'avur asher (27:10) and lema'an with an imperfect (27:25) all "so that ... may".
- gedi izzim (27:9, 16) "goat kid". laqach of what is fetched from the flock (27:9, 13, 14) "get".
- sa'ir (27:11, 23) "hairy"; chalaq (27:11) "smooth"; chelqah (27:16) "the smooth part".
- mush, mashash (27:12, 21, 22) "feel" ("grope" stays for mishshesh, Job 5:14). meta'tea (27:12) "mocker".
- chamudot of garments (27:15) "best". hilbish with a person and with al (27:15, 16) "put on", one English for both.
- nagash (27:21-27) "come close"; higgish (27:25) "bring close", leaving "come near" for qarav (27:41); 18:23 has "approach".
- attah zeh (27:21, 24) "you ... really".
- nashaq le- (27:26, 27) "kiss". reach of garments and of a field (27:27) "scent", as at Job 14:9.
- tal "dew". mishmannim (27:28, 39) "richness", kept distinct from chelev "fat". rov (27:28) "plenty of". dagan "grain"; tirosh "new wine", kept distinct from yayin "wine".
- gevir (27:29, 37) "master": it shares its English with adon. le'ummim beside ammim (27:29) both "peoples", as planned (25:23).
- orerekha arur umevarakhekha barukh (27:29) "Cursed are those who curse you, and blessed are those who bless you".
- akh with a doubled verb (27:30) "only just".
- charad (27:33) "tremble"; charadah "trembling". ad-me'od (27:33, 34) "exceedingly". tse'aqah (27:34) "cry".
- gam-ani after an imperative with "me" (27:34, 38) "me too".
- haki (27:36; 29:15) "Is it because". aqav (27:36) "cheat", with one bracketed note on the name "Jacob". atsal (27:36) "set aside".
- samakh with two objects (27:37) "sustain with". efo (27:33, 37) "then", as in Job.
- min before mishmanne and tal with moshav (27:39) "away from" [alt: of] (decision log, call: ask).
- herid (the hiphil of rud; 27:40; Ps 55:2) "grow restless" [alt at 27:40: roam free] (decision log, call: close). paraq ol me'al (27:40) "break a yoke off"; ol "yoke".
- evel (27:41) "mourning", beside aval "mourn". qarav of days (27:41) "come near".
- hitnachem le- (27:42) "comfort oneself about", beside nicham "comfort".
- chemah (27:44) "wrath" and af (27:45) "anger", as listed; shuv of wrath and anger "turn away".
- shakhol (27:45) "be bereaved of".
- quts be- (27:46) "be sick of", kept distinct from naqat be- "loathe" (Job 10:1). benot Chet "the daughters of Heth"; benot ha'arets "the daughters of the land".

## Settled in Genesis 28
- qehal ammim (28:3; 48:4) "an assembly of peoples", qahal as listed (Deut 5:22).
- shalach of a father and a son (28:5) "send"; shillach (28:6) "send away".
- al-nashav (28:9) "in addition to his wives".
- halakh with a place and -ah, on the way there (28:10) "go toward", as at 18:22.
- paga be- of a place (28:11) "come upon"; "appeal to" stays where it is said of a request (23:8).
- mera'ashot (28:11, 18) "at his head".
- chalam (28:12) "dream", beside chalom.
- sullam (28:12; only here) "stairway" [alt: ladder] (decision log, call: close). mutstsav "set up". higgia with -ah "reach to". olim weyoredim bo "going up and coming down on it", the English of John 1:51. hashamayim (28:12, 17) "the heavens".
- nitsav alav of Yahweh (28:13) "stand beside him" [alt: above it] (decision log, call: ask).
- parats of a people (28:14) "spread out", as of herds (Job 1:10).
- shamar of God and a person (28:15, 20) "watch over". heshiv el of God and a person (28:15) "bring back to". ha'adamah hazzot "this ground".
- akhen (28:16) "Surely".
- nora (28:17) "fearsome", beside yare "be afraid". en zeh ki im "This is none other than". bet Elohim (28:17, 22) "the house of God". sha'ar hashamayim "the gate of the heavens".
- matstsevah (28:18, 22; 31:13, 45; 35:14, 20) "pillar": it shares its English with ammud and netsiv. sim matstsevah "set up as a pillar". yatsaq shemen "pour oil"; rosh of a stone "top".
- we'ulam (28:19) "but", as listed; larishonah "at the first", as barishonah (13:4).
- nadar neder (28:20; 31:13) "make a vow".
- beged of what one wears, as a whole (28:20) "clothing"; lavash with no object named "wear".
- shuv beshalom (28:21) "return in peace".
- hayah li lelohim (28:21) "be God to me", as at 17:7-8.
- isser (28:22) "give a tenth", beside ma'aser "a tenth" (14:20).
- Names: Mahalath; Luz.

## Settled in Genesis 29
- nasa raglav (29:1; only here) "lift one's feet": the picture is kept, nasa as listed. bene qedem "the people of the east", as at Job 1:3.
- eder (29:2, 3, 8; 30:40) "flock"; tson beside it "sheep", as at 26:14: edre-tson "flocks of sheep".
- ravats of flocks (29:2) "lie".
- galal me'al (29:3, 8, 10) "roll off". pi habbe'er "the mouth of the well". heshiv of a stone (29:3) "put back".
- hashalom lo (29:6; 43:27) "Is it well with him?"; shalom in answer "It is well".
- hayyom gadol (29:7) "the day is long".
- ra'ah of a shepherd, with the flock as its object (29:7) "graze"; ro'ah (29:9) "shepherd", of a woman too.
- shema (29:13) "news". chibbeq "embrace". nishsheq (the piel; 29:13) "kiss", as nashaq. sipper "recount", as listed.
- atsmi uvsari (29:14) "my bone and my flesh", singular as at Job 2:5.
- chodesh yamim (29:14) "a full month".
- avad of Jacob and Laban (29:15-30) "serve"; avad im (29:25, 27, 30) "serve with"; avodah (29:27) "service". maskoret (29:15; 31:7, 41) "wages".
- haggedolah, haqqetannah of two daughters (29:16, 18) "the older", "the younger"; hatse'irah, habbekhirah (29:26) "the younger", "the firstborn", as at 19:31.
- rakh of eyes (29:17) "tender" [alt: weak] (decision log, call: ask). yefat-to'ar (29:17; 39:6) "beautiful in form".
- havah with an object (29:21) "Give me", as at Job 6:22.
- rimmah (29:25) "deceive", beside mirmah "deceit" (27:35): it shares its English with hishshi (3:13).
- shevua zot (29:27, 28) "this one's week"; zot ... zot (29:27) "this one ... that one". mille of a week "complete", beside male "be completed".
- senu'ah of a wife (29:31, 33; Deut 21:15) "hated", not softened. patach rechem (29:31; 30:22) "open the womb".
- ra'ah be'oni (29:32) "see the affliction of", so that it is heard with "saw" (29:31); ra'ah be- elsewhere "look on" (21:16).
- nilvah el (29:34; Num 18:2) "be joined to". hodah (29:35; 49:8) "praise": it shares its English with hillel. amad min with an infinitive (29:35; 30:9) "stop".
- Names: Rachel, Leah, Zilpah, Bilhah, Reuben, Simeon, Levi, Judah.

## Settled in Genesis 30
- qinne be- (30:1; 37:11) "envy", as with an object (26:14). banim in Rachel's demand (30:1) "sons", heard with "a son" (30:6).
- charah af be- (30:2; 39:19) "anger burned against", charah of anger as at Job 19:11.
- tachat Elohim (30:2; 50:19) "in the place of God". peri-beten (30:2; Deut 7:13) "the fruit of the womb". mana min "keep back from", as listed.
- yalad al-birkayim (30:3; 50:23) "give birth on the knees of": the picture is kept.
- yalad le- with ben (30:5-19) "bear ... a son", as at 21:2.
- dan with a person as its object, of God taking up her case (30:6; Ps 54:1) "judge in favor of"; elsewhere it stays "judge" (15:14).
- naftulim, niftal im (30:8; only here) "wrestlings", "wrestle with": naftule Elohim "wrestlings of God" [alt: mighty wrestlings] (decision log, call: clear). ne'evaq (32:24, 25) and sarah (32:28) are other verbs. yakhol with nothing after it (30:8; 32:28) "prevail".
- begad (30:11, the Ketiv) "What good fortune!": gad "good fortune" (Isa 65:11).
- be'oshri (30:13) "How happy I am!"; ishsher "call happy", as ashre. banot with no article (30:13; Song 6:9) "women".
- duda'im (30:14-16; Song 7:13) "mandrakes". qetsir-chittim "the wheat harvest".
- hame'at with an infinitive (30:15; Isa 7:13) "Is ... a small thing". tachat of a price (30:15) "in exchange for".
- sakhar, the verb (30:16) "hire". sakhar, the noun, of what is paid for work (30:18, 28, 32, 33; 31:8) "pay", kept distinct from maskoret "wages"; "reward" stays at 15:1.
- zavad, zeved (30:20; only here) "endow", "gift". zaval (30:20; only here) "make one's home with" [alt: honor] (decision log, call: close).
- cherpah of a state that brings scorn (30:23; 34:14) "disgrace": asaf cherpah (30:23; Isa 4:1) "take away disgrace". "Insult" stays where it is a thing said (Job 16:10).
- hosif le- (30:24) "add to".
- nichesh (30:27; 44:5, 15) "learn by divination".
- naqav sakhar al (30:28) "name one's pay to".
- parats larov (30:30) "spread out into a multitude"; parats of a man (30:43) "spread out", as at 28:14. leragli (30:30) "wherever I set foot".
- tson all through chapter 30 "flock": it covers the sheep and the goats. kesavim (30:32, 33, 35, 40) "sheep", beside izzim "goats"; seh there (30:32) "animal". tayish (30:35; 32:14) "male goat"; izzim beside it "female goats".
- naqod "speckled"; talu "spotted"; aqod "striped"; chum (only in this chapter) "dark".
- anah be- of what speaks for a person (30:33) "testify for"; elsewhere "testify against" (Deut 5:20). beyom machar (30:33) "on a day to come".
- hen lu yehi (30:34) "Look, may it be".
- derekh with a number of days (30:36; 31:23) "a journey of three days", "of seven days". hannotarot (30:36) "the rest of".
- maqqel (30:37-41) "stick". livneh (30:37; Hos 4:13) "poplar"; luz "almond" (it shares its English with shaqed); ermon (30:37; Ezek 31:8) "plane tree". lach "fresh". pitsel "peel"; petsalot "strips"; machsof "laying bare".
- rehatim (30:38, 41; Exod 2:16) "channels", beside shoqet "trough": shiqatot hammayim "the water troughs".
- yacham, yicham of the flock (30:38, 39, 41; 31:10) "breed".
- mequshsharot, qeshurim (30:41, 42) "sturdy"; he'etif, atufim (30:42) "be feeble", "feeble".
- Names: Dan, Naphtali, Gad, Asher, Issachar, Zebulun, Dinah, Joseph.

## Settled in Genesis 31
- kavod of what a man has gained (31:1) "wealth"; osher (31:16) "riches", beside he'eshir "make rich".
- kitmol shilshom (31:2, 5) "as before".
- hetel be- (31:7) "fool", as at Job 13:9. hechelif (31:7, 41) "change"; aseret monim "ten times".
- hitsil of herds and riches taken from one man and given to another (31:9, 16) "take away"; "rescue" stays at Job 5:19.
- attud (31:10, 12) "he-goat", kept distinct from tayish "male goat" (30:35). alah al of a male animal "mount". barod (31:10, 12; Zech 6:3, 6) "dappled".
- ha'el Bet-El (31:13) "the God of Bethel". mashach "anoint".
- cheleq wenachalah (31:14) "a share and an inheritance", cheleq as at 14:24. nokhriyyot "foreigners".
- banim in chapter 31: of Jacob's household (31:16, 17) "children"; beside "daughters", of what is Laban's (31:28, 43, 55) "sons".
- nahag of herds (31:18) "drive"; nihag of people (31:26) "drive off". miqneh qinyano (31:18) "the herds he had acquired".
- gazaz of a flock (31:19) "shear" ("shave" of the head, Job 1:20).
- terafim (31:19, 34, 35) "the teraphim": the name, as English has it, like the cherubim and the Nephilim; the chapter itself calls them gods (31:30, 32).
- ganav lev, and ganav with a person as its object (31:20, 26, 27) "deceive", with one bracketed note at 31:20; it shares its English with rimmah (29:25).
- hannahar by itself (31:21) "the River".
- hidbiq with a person as its object (31:23) "catch up with"; hissig (31:25) "overtake".
- mittov ad-ra after a negative (31:24, 29) "either good or bad". hishamer lekha pen "be careful that ... not" (24:6); hishamer lekha min with an infinitive (31:29) "be careful not to".
- taqa ohel (31:25) "pitch a tent": it shares its English with natah ohel.
- shevuyot cherev (31:26) "captives of the sword". nechba with an infinitive (31:27) "in secret". shir "song". natash with an infinitive (31:28) "let". hiskil aso "do a foolish thing".
- yesh le'el yadi (31:29) "it is in the power of my hand". hera im (31:7) and asah im ... ra (31:29) "do harm"; lera'ah (31:52) "for harm".
- nikhsaf le- (31:30) "yearn for", as kasaf (Job 14:15).
- pen at the head of a thing said to oneself (31:31) "might". gazal "seize", as at Job 20:19.
- kar haggamal (31:34) "the camel's saddle". mishshesh with an object (31:34, 37) "grope through". chippes "search".
- charah be'ene (31:35; 45:5) "anger in the eyes of". derekh nashim li "the way of women is on me", as at 18:11.
- riv be- (31:36) "quarrel with". pesha of a wrong that one man charges another with (31:36; Exod 22:9) "offense"; "rebellion" stays elsewhere. dalaq achare (31:36; 1 Sam 17:53) "chase hotly after".
- kelim (31:37) "things". hokhiach ben (31:37) "decide between"; with nothing after it (31:42) "give one's decision".
- rachel "ewe"; ez "female goat"; ayil "ram". terefah (31:39) "a torn animal". chitte (31:39) "bear the loss of". biqqesh miyyad "require from the hand of", beside darash miyyad "demand from the hand of" (9:5).
- chorev (31:40) "heat": it shares its English with chom. qerach of the cold of night (31:40; Jer 36:30) "frost"; "ice" stays at Job 6:16. nadad of sleep "wander".
- pachad Yitschaq (31:42, 53) "the Dread of Isaac", pachad as listed. hayah le- of God and a person (31:42; Ps 124:1) "be on the side of". reqam "empty-handed".
- asah le- in welivnotay mah-e'eseh la'elleh (31:43) "do for" [alt: do to] (decision log, call: close).
- hayah le'ed (31:44) "serve as a witness". herim of a stone (31:45) "raise". liqqet "gather". yarah of a pillar (31:51) "set up": it shares its English with sim matstsevah.
- avar et of a mark set between two sides (31:52) "cross over".
- elohim with a plural verb in Laban's oath (31:53): elohe Nachor "the god of Nahor" and elohe avihem "the gods of their father" [alt: the God of Nahor, the God of their father] (decision log, call: ask).
- zavach zevach (31:54) "offer a sacrifice". akhal lechem "eat bread".
- Names: Gilead, Jegar-Sahadutha, Galeed, Mizpah.

## Settled in Genesis 32
- machaneh (32:2, 7, 8, 10, 21; 33:8) "camp": machaneh Elohim "God's camp"; shene machanot "two camps".
- mal'akhim of men who are sent (32:3, 6) "messengers", with one bracketed note at 32:3 that it is the word for "angels" (32:1).
- paga be- of persons (32:1) "meet": it shares its English with pagash (32:17; 33:8).
- lefanav, lefanay of what is sent on or goes first (32:3, 16, 17, 20) and al-panav (32:21) "ahead of".
- koh tomerun (32:4) "This is what you are to say"; koh amar "This is what ... says".
- achar, the simple form (32:4) "delay", as ichar (24:56).
- wayehi li (32:5) "I have come to have", as at 12:16; shor wachamor "oxen and donkeys".
- wayyetser lo (32:7) "he was in distress", as at Job 20:22. chatsah (32:7; 33:1) "divide": it shares its English with palag.
- hayah lifleitah (32:8) "escape", beside palit (14:13).
- hetiv im (32:9, 12) "do good to".
- qatonti min (32:10) "I am too small for". chasadim, the plural (32:10) "acts of faithful love".
- maqqel of what a traveler carries (32:10) "staff"; "stick" stays at 30:37.
- hitsil of a person (32:11) "rescue", as at Job 5:19; the niphal (32:30) "be rescued".
- em al-banim (32:11; Hos 10:14) "mother with children".
- chol hayyam (32:12) "the sand of the sea".
- minchah of what is given to a person (32:13, 18, 20, 21; 33:10; 43:11) "present"; "offering" stays where it is brought to God (4:3), and "gift" for mattanah.
- par "bull". ayir beside aton (32:15) "male donkey" ("colt" at Job 11:12).
- eder of goats, sheep, camels, cattle and donkeys (32:16, 19) "herd"; "flock" stays where it is sheep (29:2).
- rewach (32:16) "space".
- kipper panim (32:20) "appease"; nasa panim of a person who is received (32:20) "accept": the idioms are translated by their meaning, with one bracketed note on "face".
- ma'avar (32:22) "ford". he'evir of people and goods (32:23) "bring across". nachal of the Jabbok (32:23) "torrent".
- notar (32:24) "be left": it shares its English with nish'ar (32:8).
- ne'evaq (32:24, 25; only here) "wrestle": it shares its English with niftal (30:8).
- yakhol le- (32:25) "prevail against".
- kaf hayyarekh (32:25, 32) "the socket of the thigh". yaqa (32:25) "be put out of joint".
- shillach of one who is held (32:26) "let go".
- sarah (32:28; Hos 12:3) "struggle". elohim beside anashim (32:28) "God" [alt: divine beings] (decision log, call: clear).
- panim el-panim (32:30) "face to face", as at Deut 5:4.
- zarach le- (32:31) "rise on". tsala (32:31) "limp".
- bene Yisra'el (32:32) "the sons of Israel", as "the sons of Heth" and "the sons of Ammon".
- gid hannasheh (32:32; nasheh only here) "the sinew of the hip".
- Names: Mahanaim, the Jabbok, Israel; Peniel (32:30) and Penuel (32:31), each as the Hebrew spells it.

## Settled in Genesis 33
- acharonim, twice (33:2) "after them", beside rishonah "first".
- nafal al-tsavvar (33:4; 45:14; 46:29) "fall on the neck of": the gesture is kept.
- mi-elleh lakh (33:5) "Who are these to you?"; mi lekha with a thing (33:8) "What is ... of yours".
- chanan with God as its subject (33:5, 11) "favor", heard with chen "favor" (33:8, 10, 15): with two objects "favor ... with". "Have pity on" stays in Job (19:21).
- yesh-li rav (33:9) "I have plenty"; yesh-li-khol (33:11) "I have everything".
- al-na by itself (33:10) "Please, no", as at 19:18.
- ratsah with a person as its object (33:10) "accept": it shares its English with nasa panim (32:20).
- berakhah of a thing given (33:11; 1 Sam 25:27) "blessing": the word is kept, so that it is heard with 27:35-36.
- patsar be- with no me'od (33:11) "press".
- lenegdekha of one who travels with another (33:12) "in front of you".
- alot of flocks and cattle (33:13; Isa 40:11) "nursing": it shares its English with meniqot (32:15). alay "in my care".
- dafaq of herds (33:13) "drive hard".
- hitnahel (33:14; only here) "move along"; le'itti "slowly", beside la'at "gentle".
- leregel (33:14) "at the pace of": the idiom is translated by its meaning.
- melakhah of animals that are driven (33:14) "livestock": it shares its English with behemah; "work" stays at 2:2-3.
- hitsig im (33:15) "leave with".
- lammah zeh emtsa-chen (33:15) "Why is that? Let me find favor" [alt: Why is it that I should find favor] (decision log, call: close).
- sukkah (33:17) "shelter", with one bracketed note on the name "Succoth".
- shalem of one who arrives (33:18) "safely" [alt: the name Salem] (decision log, call: clear). ir Shekhem "the city of Shechem".
- chanah et-pene (33:18) "camp in front of".
- chelqat hassadeh (33:19; Josh 24:32; Ruth 2:3) "the plot of the field".
- qesitah (33:19; Josh 24:32; Job 42:11) "kesitah": the unit is kept, as sheqel is.
- hitsiv mizbeach (33:20) "set up an altar", beside banah "build".
- El Elohe Yisra'el (33:20) "God, the God of Israel".
- Names: Succoth, Hamor.

## Settled in Genesis 34
- ra'ah be- of people one goes out to (34:1) "see"; "look on" stays at 21:16.
- innah of a man and a woman (34:2; Deut 22:24, 29; 2 Sam 13:12, 14) "violate": the word does not itself say force; "afflict" stays elsewhere (15:13; 16:6; 31:50).
- dibber al-lev (34:3; 50:21) "speak to the heart of": the picture is kept. davaq nefesh be- (34:3) "his soul clung to", davaq as at 2:24.
- yaldah (34:4) "girl": it shares its English with almah.
- timme (34:5, 13, 27) "defile", beside tame "unclean": it shares its English with miaino.
- keshom'am (34:7) with "came in from the field", as the Hebrew accents divide the verse [alt: with "the men were pained"] (decision log, call: close).
- nevalah (34:7; Deut 22:21; Josh 7:15) "outrage": the noun of naval "foolish" (Job 2:10). asah nevalah beYisra'el "do an outrage in Israel" [alt at 34:7: against Israel] (decision log, call: clear).
- wekhen lo ye'aseh (34:7; 2 Sam 13:12) "and such a thing is not done".
- chashaq nefesh be- (34:8) "his soul is set on".
- hitchatten et (34:9) "intermarry with", beside chatan "son-in-law".
- sachar with a land as its object (34:10, 21; 42:34) "trade in", beside socher "merchant" [alt at 34:10: move about in] (decision log, call: clear).
- ne'echaz be- (34:10; 47:27) "take holdings in", beside achuzzah "holding".
- mohar (34:12; Exod 22:17) "bride price". mattan (34:12) "gift": it shares its English with mattanah. hirbah al ... me'od (34:12) "put a very great ... on".
- ish asher-lo orlah (34:14) "a man who has a foreskin"; arel stays "uncircumcised".
- ne'ot le- (34:15, 22, 23) "consent to"; akh-bezot (34:15, 22) "Only on this condition".
- yatav be'ene (34:18; 41:37) "be good in the eyes of", as tov be'ene.
- chafets be- of a person (34:19) "delight in"; "wish" stays in Job (9:3).
- nikhbad mikkol (34:19) "the most honored of all".
- shalem im (34:21) "at peace with", with one bracketed note that it is the word for "safely" (33:18).
- rachavat yadayim (34:21; Judg 18:10) "broad on every side", rachav as at Job 11:9.
- qinyan (34:23; 36:6) "what one has acquired", as at 31:18.
- kol-yots'e sha'ar iro (34:24) "all who went out of the gate of his city", as ba'e sha'ar iro (23:10, 18).
- ko'ev (34:25) "in pain", as at Job 14:22.
- betach (34:25) "in security", as at Job 11:18, taken with the ones who came [alt: of the city, "while it felt secure"] (decision log, call: close).
- ba al of an attack (34:25) "come against"; with the slain (34:27) "come upon".
- harag lefi-charev (34:26) "kill with the edge of the sword", as at Job 1:15.
- chalal (34:27) "the slain". bazaz (34:27, 29) "plunder".
- taf (34:29; 43:8) "little ones", kept distinct from yeled "child". shavah (34:29) "take captive", as nishbah (14:14).
- babbayit, a collective (34:29) "in the houses".
- akhar (34:30; Josh 7:25) "bring trouble on". hiv'ish be- (34:30; 1 Sam 27:12) "make stink among": the picture is kept.
- yoshev ha'arets (34:30; 50:11) "those who live in the land".
- mete mispar (34:30; Deut 4:27) "few in number", as the Job 16 line planned.
- nishmad (34:30) "be destroyed": it shares its English with ibbed.
- zonah (34:31; 38:15) "prostitute". asah et of how a person is dealt with (34:31) "treat".
- Names: hachiwwi (34:2) "the Hivite", as at 10:17.

## Settled in Genesis 35
- elohe hannekhar (35:2, 4; Josh 24:20, 23) "the foreign gods", beside ben-nekhar "foreigner".
- hittaher (35:2) "make oneself clean", beside tahor "clean".
- simlot, the plural, of what a person wears (35:2; 37:34; 41:14) "clothes"; "cloak" stays for the one garment (9:23). hechelif of clothes "change", as at 31:7.
- tsarah, the singular (35:3; 42:21) "distress": yom tsarati "the day of my distress".
- taman (35:4) "hide", as at Job 3:16.
- elah (35:4; Isa 6:13) "terebinth", kept distinct from elon and allon "oak".
- nasa with nothing after it (35:5) "set out"; (35:21) "travel on".
- chittah (35:5; only here) "terror", as chat (9:2): chittat Elohim "the terror of God".
- niglah el (35:7) "reveal oneself to", beside gillah "uncover". ha'elohim with a plural verb (35:7) "God" [alt: the divine beings] (decision log, call: clear).
- El Bet-El (35:7) "God of Bethel", as ha'el Bet-El (31:13).
- mittachat le- (35:8) "below".
- qehal goyim (35:11) "an assembly of nations", beside qehal ammim "an assembly of peoples" (28:3). hayah min (35:11) "be from", as at 17:16.
- chalatsayim (35:11; 1 Kgs 8:19) "loins", kept distinct from me'ayim "body" (15:4) and motnayim "waist".
- hitsiv matstsevah (35:14, 20) "set up a pillar"; matstsevet even "a pillar of stone".
- nesekh "drink offering"; nasakh nesekh (35:14) "pour out a drink offering", beside yatsaq "pour".
- kivrat ha'arets (35:16; 48:7; 2 Kgs 5:19) "a stretch of land".
- qishshah belidtah, hiqshah belidtah (35:16, 17) "have hard labor", "be in hard labor".
- meyalledet (35:17; 38:28; Exod 1:15) "midwife".
- yatsa of nefesh (35:18) "go out": nefesh there "life".
- qevurah (35:20) "grave": it shares its English with qever.
- mehal'ah le- (35:21) "beyond".
- seva yamim (35:29; Job 42:17) "full of days".
- 35:22 has a paragraph break inside the verse in the Hebrew text, set as at Deut 5:21.
- Names: Deborah, Allon-Bacuth, Ephrath, Bethlehem, Ben-Oni, Benjamin, Migdal-Eder.

## Settled in Genesis 36
- alluf (36:15-43; Exod 15:15) "chief": it shares its English with rosh of a leader (Job 12:24); nasi stays "chieftain". alluf with a name "chief Teman" [alt at 36:40: the chief of Timna] (decision log, call: clear). le'allufehem (36:30) "chief by chief".
- Esav hu Edom (36:1) "Esau (that is, Edom)"; as a sentence of its own (36:8) "Esau is Edom"; hu Edom, hu Esav at the end of a list (36:19, 43) "That is Edom", "That is Esau".
- nafshot beto (36:6) "the people of his household", as hannefesh (12:5).
- mippene of a person one moves away from (36:6) "away from"; of a cause (36:7) it stays "because of".
- rav min with an infinitive (36:7) "too many to".
- yoshve ha'arets (36:20) "those who lived in the land", as at 34:30.
- hayyemim (36:24; only here) "the hot springs" [alt: the mules, or the Emim] (decision log, call: ask).
- malakh "reign"; malakh tachtav (36:33-39) "reign in his place". lifne melokh-melekh livne Yisra'el (36:31) "before any king of the sons of Israel reigned".
- limqomotam (36:40) "according to their places"; lemoshvotam (36:43) "according to the places where they lived", as moshav (10:30); erets achuzzatam "the land of their holding".
- 36:2 bat-Tsiv'on "the daughter of Zibeon" [alt: the son of Zibeon] (decision log, call: clear); the bracket is not repeated at 36:14. 36:26 Dishan [alt: Dishon] (decision log, call: clear).
- The Ketiv Ye'ish (36:5, 14) "Jeish", with one bracketed note at 36:5; Ye'ush as written (36:18) "Jeush".
- Sha'ul of the king of Edom (36:37, 38) "Shaul", his usual English name; it is the Hebrew name of Saul, king of Israel (Obay can switch it).
- Names: Oholibamah, Anah, Zibeon, Reuel, Jalam; Teman, Omar, Zepho, Gatam, Kenaz, Timna, Amalek; Nahath, Zerah, Shammah, Mizzah; Lotan, Shobal, Dishon, Ezer, Dishan; Hori, Hemam; Alvan, Manahath, Ebal, Shepho, Onam; Aiah; Hemdan, Eshban, Ithran, Cheran; Bilhan, Zaavan, Akan; Aran; Beor, Dinhabah, Bozrah, Husham, Bedad, Avith, Samlah, Masrekah, Baal-Hanan, Achbor, Hadar, Pau, Mehetabel, Matred, Me-Zahab; Alvah, Jetheth, Elah, Pinon, Mibzar, Magdiel, Iram. hattemani (36:34) "the Temanites"; Rechovot hannahar (36:37) "Rehoboth on the River". Already published and spelled the same: Adah (4:19), Eliphaz (Job 2:11), Korah (Jude 11), Uz (10:23), Bela (14:2), Jobab (10:29), Hadad (25:15, where the Hebrew name begins with another letter).

## Settled in Genesis 37
- ketonet passim (37:3, 23, 32; 2 Sam 13:18, 19) "a long tunic" [alt at 37:3: a tunic of many colors] (decision log, call: ask); the bracket is not repeated at 37:23, 32.
- dibbah (37:2; Num 13:32) "report": dibbatam ra'ah "a bad report about them".
- dibber leshalom (37:4) "speak peaceably to". ra'ah et-shelom (37:14) "see whether it is well with", as hashalom lo (29:6).
- alummah (37:7) "sheaf"; illem "bind". savav of the sheaves "gather around".
- An infinitive absolute with its own verb in a question (37:8, 10) "really", as at 24:5.
- ga'ar be- (37:10) "rebuke": it shares its English with hokhiach (21:25).
- shamar et-haddavar (37:11) "keep the matter in mind".
- ra'ah of shepherds, with no object (37:13, 16) "shepherd"; with the flock as its object (37:12) "graze", as at 29:7.
- heshiv davar (37:14) "bring back word".
- biqqesh (37:15, 16) "look for", as at Job 10:6.
- hitnakkel et (37:18) "plot against".
- ba'al hachalomot (37:19) "dreamer": the idiom is translated by its meaning. hallazeh "that", as at 24:65.
- bor (37:20-29; 40:15; 41:14) "pit": it shares its English with shachat (Job 9:31).
- chayyah ra'ah (37:20, 33; Lev 26:6) "a savage animal".
- hikkah nefesh (37:21; Deut 19:6, 11) "take the life of": the idiom is translated by its meaning.
- hifshit with two objects (37:23) "strip ... of", as at Job 19:9.
- orchah (37:25) "caravan", as at Job 6:19. nekhot (37:25; 43:11) "gum"; tsori "balm"; lot "resin".
- betsa (37:26) "profit". kissah dam "cover blood", as at Job 16:18.
- mashakh with he'elah (37:28) "pull up", "lift out". esrim kasef "twenty pieces of silver", as at 20:16.
- begadim (37:29) "garments", as at 27:15; simlot (37:34) "clothes", as planned.
- hayyeled enennu (37:30) "The child is gone": 42:13, 36 have the same word of Joseph.
- se'ir izzim (37:31) "male goat": it shares its English with tayish (30:35).
- hakker-na (37:32; 38:25) "Recognize it, please", hikkir as listed.
- tarof toraf (37:33; 44:28) "has certainly been torn to pieces", taraf as listed.
- hit'abbel al (37:34) "mourn for"; avel (37:35) "mourning". hitnachem (37:35) "be comforted", the English of the niphal (24:67).
- saris (37:36; 39:1; 40:2, 7) "court official" [alt at 37:36: eunuch] (decision log, call: clear), kept distinct from sar "official" (12:15). sar hattabbachim (37:36; 39:1; 40:3) "the commander of the guard", sar as at 21:22.
- hammedanim (37:36) "the Medanites" [alt: the Midianites] (decision log, call: clear).
- Names: Dothan, Potiphar; hayyishme'elim "the Ishmaelites"; midyanim (37:28) "Midianite".

## Settled in Genesis 38
- natah ad, natah el of a person (38:1, 16) "turn aside to": it shares its English with sur el (19:2-3).
- wehayah bikhziv (38:5) "And he was at Chezib" [alt: And she was at Chezib when she gave birth to them] (decision log, call: clear).
- ra be'ene YHWH of a person and of what he did (38:7, 10) "evil in the eyes of Yahweh", with one bracketed note at 38:7 on the name "Er"; "bad in the eyes of" stays at 21:11 and 28:8.
- yibbem (38:8; Deut 25:5, 7) "do the duty of a husband's brother to". heqim zera le- "raise up offspring for".
- shichet artsah (38:9) "waste ... on the ground", with "his seed" supplied: zera is the word for "offspring" and for "seed". "Ruin" stays elsewhere.
- almanah "widow"; almenut "widowhood": bigde almenutah "her widow's garments". cham "father-in-law".
- wayyirbu hayyamim (38:12) "many days passed". kemishlosh chodashim (38:24) "about three months later".
- rea of Hirah (38:12, 20) "friend", as at Job 2:11 [alt at 38:12: shepherd] (decision log, call: clear); the bracket is not repeated at 38:20.
