- hesir me'al of what a person wears (38:14, 19) "take off"; "remove" stays elsewhere. hit'allef (38:14) "wrap oneself up".
- chashav le- of a person taken for what she is not (38:15) "take for"; "count as" stays at 31:15.
- eravon (38:17, 18, 20) "pledge".
- chotam, chotemet "seal"; petil "cord"; matteh "staff": it shares its English with maqqel (32:10).
- qedeshah (38:21, 22; Deut 23:17; Hos 4:14) "shrine prostitute", kept distinct from zonah "prostitute" (38:15), with one bracketed note at 38:21. zanah (38:24) "prostitute oneself"; zenunim "prostitution".
- hayah lavuz (38:23) "be held in contempt", buz as listed.
- saraf of a person (38:24) "burn".
- tsadaq min (38:26; Ezek 16:52) "be more righteous than" [alt at 38:26: She is in the right; it is by me] (decision log, call: clear).
- natan yad (38:28) "put out a hand". shani (38:28, 30) "scarlet thread", with "thread" supplied. qashar al "tie on".
- parats perets (38:29) "make a breach", perets as at Job 16:14, with one bracketed note on the name "Perez".
- Names: Hirah, Shua, Er, Onan, Tamar, Chezib, Enaim, Perez; adullami "Adullamite". Shelah (38:5) is not the name of 10:24, which ends with another letter, and Timnah (38:12) is not the Timna of 36:12; English has one spelling for each pair. Zerah as at 36:13.

## Settled in Genesis 39
- qanah of a person (39:1) "buy", as of land (25:10).
- matsliach of a person (39:2) "successful"; with what he does as its object (39:3, 23) "make succeed", as at 24:21.
- sheret (39:4; 40:4) "attend", kept distinct from avad "serve": it shares its English with paqad "attend to" (21:1).
- hifqid al (39:4, 5) "put in charge of"; hifqid be- (39:5) "put in charge in".
- natan beyad (39:4, 8, 22) "give into the hand of"; azav beyad (39:6, 12, 13) "leave in the hand of".
- lo yada itto, itti (39:6, 8) "with him there", "with me here ... give no thought to": yada of what a person keeps track of, with "there" and "here" supplied.
- yefeh-to'ar wifeh mar'eh (39:6) "beautiful in form and beautiful in appearance", of a man too: the words of 29:17.
- nasa enayim el (39:7) "lift one's eyes to", as listed.
- ba'asher (39:9, 23) "because".
- yom yom (39:10) "day after day". shakhav etsel (39:10) "lie beside", kept distinct from shakhav im "lie with" (39:7, 12, 14).
- kehayyom hazzeh in a story (39:11) "on one such day".
- tafas be- (39:12) "grab by" ("handle" at 4:21).
- bo el followed by an infinitive (39:14, 17) "come to": not the idiom of 6:4.
- tsicheq be- (39:14, 17) "toy with"; "joke" stays at 19:14.
- qara beqol gadol (39:14) "call out with a loud voice". herim qol (39:15, 18) "raise one's voice", beside nasa qol "lift one's voice".
- hinniach of a thing (39:16) "let lie".
- kaddevarim ha'elleh (39:17) "in words like these"; (39:19) "the kind of thing".
- eved of Joseph in Egypt (39:17, 19) "slave": a man who was bought (the Job 3 line).
- 39:17 letsacheq bi with "came to me" [alt: with "you brought to us"] (decision log, call: close).
- bet hassohar (39:20-23; 40:3, 5) "prison"; sar bet-hassohar "the commander of the prison", sar as at 37:36. asir "prisoner", as listed; asur of a prisoner (39:20; 40:3, 5) "confined" (asar elsewhere "bind").
- natah chesed el (39:21) "extend faithful love to". natan chen be'ene (39:21; Exod 3:21) "give ... favor in the eyes of".
- ra'ah et of one who oversees (39:23) "look into". kol me'umah "anything at all".

## Settled in Genesis 40
- mashqeh of a man (40:1, 5, 13) "cupbearer"; ofeh "baker". sar hammashqim, sar ha'ofim (40:2-23; 41:9, 10) "the chief cupbearer", "the chief baker": sar here shares "chief" with alluf. mashqeh of his office (40:21) "cupbearing".
- qatsaf al (40:2; 41:10) "be furious with", kept distinct from charah "be angry".
- mishmar of a place where men are held (40:3, 4, 7; 41:10; 42:17) "custody".
- paqad et ... et (40:4) "assign ... to".
- yamim by itself, of a length of time (40:4) "for some time", as at 4:3.
- patar "interpret"; pitron "interpretation"; ufoter en oto (40:8; 41:15) "and there is no one to interpret it". ish kefitron chalomo (40:5; 41:11) "each dream with its own interpretation".
- zo'afim (40:6) "troubled". panim ra'im (40:7) "sad faces".
- sipper with no object (40:8) "recount it".
- sarigim (40:10, 12) "branches". parach "bud", as at Job 14:9; hivshil (40:10) "ripen"; eshkol "cluster"; anavim "grapes". sachat (40:11; only here) "squeeze". kos "cup".
- natan al-kaf (40:11, 21) "put on the palm of", beside natan beyad (40:13) "give into the hand of".
- nasa rosh (40:13, 19, 20) "lift the head of": the picture is kept all three times.
- be'od sheloshet yamim (40:13, 19) "In three more days", as at 7:4.
- heshiv al-ken (40:13; 41:13) "restore to one's post"; heshiv al (40:21) "restore to".
- kammishpat harishon (40:13) "according to the former practice".
- zakar ... ittekha (40:14) "keep in your memory"; hizkir el (40:14) "mention to", with one bracketed note; zakar (40:23) "remember" and shakhach "forget", as listed.
- chesed between the cupbearer and Joseph (40:14) "loyalty", as at 20:13.
- gunnav of a person (40:15) "be stolen".
- sal (40:16-18) "basket". salle chori (40:16; chori only here) "baskets of white bread" [alt: open-weave baskets] (decision log, call: close).
- ma'aseh ofeh (40:17) "the work of a baker". elyon of a basket "top".
- me'al with a suffix (40:17, 19) "from off".
- talah al-ets (40:19; Deut 21:22) "hang on a tree".
- yom hulledet (40:20) "birthday".
